## 二、数据集描述（平台原文）

> ⚠️ 平台目录描述存在**错配**（已实测：insiders1 / imbalance5 的描述指向无关数据集）。以「五、全字段明细」的真实字段语义为准。

This dataset provides proprietary, quantitative ratings that measure non-financial statement factors—such as management quality, environmental impact, employee well-being, diversity, and trust—across 4,500 global securities. The ratings are structured around six pillars: management, health, wealth, earth, equality, and trust, and are designed to capture intangible drivers of cash flow, risk, and valuation. By quantifying ESG and sustainability factors into a 0-100 score, the dataset enables investors to assess companies’ human impact and profit potential, identify leaders and laggards in sustainability, and integrate these insights into investment models.
